### 第 388 页

P-44.2.2.2.3 Seniority criteria for fused ring systems given below are applied successively until no alternatives remain.
The criteria are illustrated in P-44.2.2.2.3.1 through P-44.2.2.2.3.5. The senior fused ring system:
(a) has the larger individual ring component at first point of difference when their ring sizes are compared in order
of decreasing size;
(b) has the greater number of rings in a horizontal row;
(c) has the lower letter(s) in the fusion descriptor when compared as a set; letters omitted in names are considered in
the application of this criterion;
(d) has the lower number(s) in the fusion descriptor, in the order of appearance in the name; locants omitted in
names are considered in application of this criterion;
(e) has the senior ring system component according to P-25.8 when its components are compared in order of
decreasing seniority.
Further criteria applicable to fused ring systems are found in P-44.4.
P-44.2.2.2.3.1 The senior fused ring system has the larger individual ring component at first point of difference when
their ring sizes are compared in order of decreasing size [criterion (a) in P-44.2.2.2.3].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
azulene (PIN) 萘 (PIN)
[the ring size '7' in the ring set '7,5' is larger than '6' in the ring set '6,6']
P-44.2.2.2.3.2 The senior fused ring system has the greater number of rings in a horizontal row [criterion (b) in
P-44.2.2.2.3].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
(1)
>
蒽 (PIN) 菲 (PIN)
[three rings in the horizontal row is greater than two]
(2)
>
naphtho[1,2-g]喹啉 (PIN) naphtho[2,1-f]喹啉 (PIN)
[three rings in the horizontal row is greater than two]
P-44.2.2.2.3.3 The senior fused ring system has the lower letter(s) alphabetically in the fusion descriptor when
compared as a set; letters omitted in names are considered in the application of this criterion [criterion (c) in
P-44.2.2.2.3].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
5H-[1,3]dioxolo[4,5-c][1,2]oxa磷杂环戊二烯 (PIN) 5H-[1,3]dioxolo[4,5-d][1,2]oxa磷杂环戊二烯 (PIN)
[the letter 'c' in the fusion descriptor is lower alphabetically than 'd']


### 第 389 页

P-44.2.2.2.3.4 The senior fused ring system has the lower set of numbers in the fusion descriptor, in the order of
appearance in a name; locants omitted in names are taken into consideration in application of this criterion [criterion (d)
in P-44.2.2.2.3]. Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
naphtho[1,2-f]喹啉 (PIN) naphtho[2,1-f]喹啉 (PIN)
[the locant set '1,2' in the fusion descriptor is lower than '2,1']
P-44.2.2.2.3.5 The senior fused ring system has the senior ring system component according to P-25.8 when its
components are compared in order of decreasing seniority [criterion (e) in P-44.2.2.2.3].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
naphtho[2,3-f]喹啉 (PIN) naphtho[2,3-f]异喹啉 (PIN)
[the senior ring system 喹啉 is senior to 异喹啉 (see P-25.2.1, Table 2.8)]
P-44.2.2.2.4 Seniority criteria for bridged fused ring systems given below are applied successively until no alternatives
remain. The criteria are illustrated in P-44.2.2.2.4.1 through P-44.2.2.2.4.14. The senior bridged fused ring system:
(a) has the bridged ring system with the greater number of rings before bridging;
(b) is the bridged ring system with the greater number of ring atoms before bridging;
(c)!is the bridged ring system with the fewer 杂原子 in the fused ring system before bridging;
(d) is the bridged ring system with the senior fused ring system before bridging according to P-44-2.2.2.3;
(e) has the lower set of locants for bridge attachments;
(f) has the lower locant(s) for 杂原子 in bridges, without regard to kind;
(g) has the lower locant(s) for 杂原子 in bridges, in the order: F > Cl > Br > I > O > S > Se > Te > N > P > As
> Sb > Bi > Si > Ge > Sn > Pb > B > Al > Ga > In > Tl;
(h) has the fewer composite bridges;
(i) has the fewer dependent bridges;
(j) has fewer atoms in dependent bridges;
(k) has the greater number of divalent bridges;
(l) has the lower set of locants for attachment of independent bridges;
(m) has the lower set of locants for attachment of dependent bridges;
(n) has the fused ring system with the greater number of noncumulative 双键 before bridging.
Further criteria applicable to bridged fused ring systems are found in P-44.4.
P-44.2.2.2.4.1 The senior bridged fused ring system has the bridged ring system with the greater number of rings before
bridging [criterion (a) in P-44.2.2.2.4].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
4,7-methanocyclopenta[a]茚 (PIN) 2H-1,4:5,8-dimethanobenzo[7]轮烯 (PIN)
[three rings in the ring system before bridging is greater than two]


### 第 390 页

P-44.2.2.2.4.2 The senior bridged fused ring system is the bridged ring system with the greater number of ring atoms
before bridging [criterion (b) in P-44.2.2.2.4].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
[fourteen ring atoms in the ring system before bridging is greater than thirteen]
P-44.2.2.2.4.3 The senior bridged fused ring system is the bridged ring system with the fewer 杂原子 in the fused
ring system before bridging [criterion (c) in P-44.2.2.2.4].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
1,4:5,8-diepoxy蒽 (PIN) 1,4:6,9-dimethanooxanthrene (PIN)
[zero 杂原子 in the fused ring system before bridging is fewer than two]
P-44.2.2.2.4.4 The senior bridged fused ring system is the bridged ring system with the senior fused ring system before
bridging according to P-44.2.2.2.3 [criterion (d) in P-44.2.2.2.4].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
4,7-methanoazulene (PIN) 1,4-methano萘 (PIN)
[azulene is senior to 萘, see P-44.2.1]
P-44.2.2.2.4.5 The senior bridged fused ring system has the lower set of locants for bridge attachments [criterion (e) in
P-44.2.2.2.4].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
1,3-methano萘 (PIN)
1,4-methano萘 (PIN)
[the locant set for bridge attachments '1,3' is lower than '1,4']
P-44.2.2.2.4.6 The senior bridged fused ring system has the lower locant(s) for 杂原子 in bridges, without regard
to kind [criterion (f) in P-44.2.2.2.4].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
5,1-(epoxyethano)octalene (PIN) 1,5-(methanooxymethano)octalene (PIN)
[the locant '13' for the bridge oxygen atom is lower than '14']
P-44.2.2.2.4.7 The senior bridged fused ring system has the lower locants for 杂原子 in bridges, in the order: F >
Cl > Br > I > O > S > Se > Te > N > P > As > Sb > Bi > Si > Ge > Sn > Pb > B > Al > Ga > In > Tl [criterion (g) in
P-44.2.2.2.4].


### 第 391 页

Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
5,1-(epoxymethanosulfano)octalene (PIN) 1,5-(epoxymethanosulfano)octalene (PIN)
[the locant '13' for the senior oxygen atom in the bridge is lower than '15']
P-44.2.2.2.4.8 The senior bridged fused ring system has the fewer composite bridges [criterion (h) in P-44.2.2.2.4].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
1,4-epoxy-5,8-ethano萘 (PIN) 1,4-(epoxymethano)-5,8-methano萘 (PIN)
[zero composite bridges is fewer than one]
P-44.2.2.2.4.9 The senior bridged fused ring system has the fewer dependent bridges [criterion (i) in P-44.2.2.2.4].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
(I) (II)
(I) 1,4-methano-10,13-pentanonaphtho[2,3-c][1]benzazocine (PIN)
is senior to
(II) 13,18-methano-6,10-pentanonaphtho[2,3-c][1]benzazocine (PIN)
[zero dependent bridges is fewer than one]
P-44.2.2.2.4.10 The senior bridged fused ring system has fewer atoms in dependent bridges [criterion (j) in
P-44.2.2.2.4].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
(I) (II)
(I) 13,18-methano-6,10-heptanonaphtho[2,3-c][1]benzazocine (PIN)
is senior to
(II) 13,18-ethano-6,10-hexanonaphtho[2,3-c][1]benzazocine (PIN)
[one atom in the dependent bridge in (I) (atom 22)
is fewer the two atoms in (II) (atoms 21 and 22)]
P-44.2.2.2.4.11 The senior bridged fused ring system has the greater number of divalent bridges [criterion (k) in
P-44.2.2.2.4].


### 第 392 页

Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
(I)
(II)
(I) 1-oxa-2,7:6,8-dimethanocycloocta[1,2,3-cd]pentalene (PIN)
is senior to
(II) 1-oxa-5,9,2-(ethane[1,1,2]triyl)cycloocta[1,2,3-cd]pentalene (PIN)
['2' divalent bridges in (I) is senior to the '1' trivalent bridge in (II)]
P-44.2.2.2.4.12 The senior bridged fused ring system has the lower set of locants for attachment of independent bridges
[criterion (l) in P-44.2.2.2.4].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
(I) (II)
(I) 13,18-methano-6,9-pentanonaphtho[2,3-c][1]benzazocine (PIN)
is senior to
(II) 13,18-methano-6,10-pentanonaphtho[2,3-c][1]benzazocine (PIN)
[the locant set '6,9' for the independent bridge in (I) is lower than the locant set '6,10' in (II)]
P-44.2.2.2.4.13 The senior bridged fused ring system has the lower set of locants for attachment of dependent bridges
[criterion (m) in P-44.2.2.2.4].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
(I) (II)
13,17-methano-6,10-pentanonaphtho[2,3-c][1]benzazocine (PIN) (I)
is senior to
13,18-methano-6,10-pentanonaphtho[2,3-c][1]benzazocine (PIN) (II)
[the locant set '13,17' for the dependent bridge in (I)
is lower than the locant set '13,18' in (II)]
P-44.2.2.2.4.14 The senior bridged fused ring system has the fused ring system with the greater number of
noncumulative 双键 before bridging [criterion (n) in P-44.2.2.2.4].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
(I) (II)
1,2,3,4,4a,9,9a,10-octahydro-9,10-ethano蒽 (PIN) (I)
is senior to
1,2,3,4,4a,8a,9,9a,10,10a-decahydro-9,10-etheno蒽 (PIN) (II)
[three noncumulative 双键 in (I) is greater than the
two noncumulative 双键 in (II)]


### 第 393 页

P-44.2.2.2.5 Seniority criteria for bridged nonfused ring systems (von Baeyer ring systems) are applied successively
until there are no alternatives remaining. These criteria are illustrated in P-44.2.2.2.5.1 through P-44.2.2.2.5.3. The
senior bridged nonfused ring system:
(a) has the lower number at the first point of difference in the descriptor set for describing ring sizes when
considered in order of citation in the name;
(b) has the lower set of bridge attachment locants (superscript locants) at the first point of difference when compared
term by term in order of increasing numerical value;
(c) has the lower set of bridge attachment locants (superscript locants) at first point of difference when compared
term by term in their order of citation in the name.
Further criteria applicable to bridged nonfused ring systems are found in P-44.4.
P-44.2.2.2.5.1 The senior bridged nonfused ring system has the lower number in the descriptor set for describing ring
sizes at the first point of difference when considered in order of citation in the name [criteria (a) in P-44.2.2.2.5].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
bicyclo[2.2.2]octane (PIN) bicyclo[3.2.1]octane (PIN)
[the ring descriptor set '2,2,2' is lower than '3,2,1']
P-44.2.2.2.5.2 The senior bridged nonfused ring system has the lower set of bridge attachment locants (superscript
locants) at the first point of difference when compared term by term in order of increasing numerical value [criteria (b)
in P-44.2.2.2.5].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
tricyclo[5.2.1.11,4]undecane (PIN) tricyclo[5.2.1.12,5]undecane (PIN)
[the bridge attachment locant set '1,4' is lower than '2,5']
P-44.2.2.2.5.3 The senior bridged nonfused ring system has the lower set of bridge attachment locants (superscript
locants) at first point of difference when compared term by term in their order of citation in the name [criterion (c) in P-
44.2.2.2.5].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
tetracyclo[5.5.2.22,5.18,12]heptadecane (PIN) tetracyclo[5.5.2.22,6.18,12]heptadecane (PIN)
[the bridge attachment locant set '2,5,8,12' is lower than '2,6,8,12']
P-44.2.2.2.6 线性（无环）环蕃
Although linear phane systems can be viewed as heteroacyclic chains, in which the 扩展组分 are 杂原子 and
the connecting atoms or chains are carbon atoms, seniority for linear (acyclic) phanes follows closely the criteria used
for cyclic phane systems (see P-44.2.2.2.2). Accordingly, the seniority criteria for linear phane systems given below are
applied successively until no alternatives remain. These criteria are illustrated in P-44.2.2.2.6.1 through P-44.2.2.2.6.10.
The senior linear phane system has:
(a) the senior 扩展组分, as defined by P-44.2.1.2 through P-44.2.1.8;
(b) most 扩展组分 in their order of seniority as defined by P-44.2.1.2 through P-44.2.1.8;
(c) the maximum number of skeletal nodes;
(d) the lower superatom locant(s) for the senior 扩展组分;


### 第 394 页

(e) the lower superatom locant(s) for all 扩展组分 as a set when compared term by term in order of
increasingvalue and then in order of citation in the name;
(f) the lower superatom locant(s) for all 扩展组分 when compared in order of citation in the name;
(g) the lower set of attachment locants considered as a set when compared term by term in order of increasing
numerical value;
(h) the lower set of attachment locants when compared term by term in their order of citation in the name;
(i) the greater number of 杂原子 introduced by 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature without regard to kind;
(j) the greater number of 杂原子 introduced by 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature first cited in the
following order: F > Cl > Br > I > O > S > Se > Te > N > P > As > Sb > Bi > Si > Ge > Sn > Pb > B > Al > Ga >
In > Tl.
Further criteria applicable to linear (acyclic) phanes are found in P-44.4.
P-44.2.2.2.6.1 The senior linear phane system has the senior 扩展组分, as defined by P-44.2.1.2. through P-44.2.1.8
[criterion (a) in P-44.2.2.2.6]
Examples:
(1)
1(4)-pyridina-3,5(1,4),7(1)-tribenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
is senior to
1(4)-silina-3,5(1,4),7(1)-tribenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
[the 扩展组分 'pyridina' is senior to the 扩展组分 'silina']
(2)
1(2)-呋喃a-3,5(1,4),7(1)-tribenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
is senior to
1(2)-thiophena-3,5(1,4),7(1)-tribenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
[the 扩展组分 '呋喃a' is senior to the 扩展组分 'thiophena']
(3)
1(2)-quinolina-7(4)-pyridina-3,5(1,4)-dibenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
is senior to
1,7(4)-dipyridina-3,5(1,4)-dibenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
[the 扩展组分 'quinolina' is senior to the 扩展组分 'pyridina']
(4)
11H-1(2)-azepina-7(4)-pyridina-3,5(1,4)-dibenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
is senior to
1(2),7(4)-dipyridina-3,5(1,4)-dibenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
[the 扩展组分 'azepina' is senior to the 扩展组分 'pyridina']


### 第 395 页

P-44.2.2.2.6.2 The senior linear phane system has the most 扩展组分 in their order of seniority as defined by P-
44.2.1.2 through P-44.2.1.8 [criterion (b) in P-44.2.2.2.6].
Examples:
(1)
1,7(4)-dipyridina-3,5(1,4)-dibenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
is senior to
1(4)-pyridina-7(2)-silina-3,5(1,4)-dibenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
[the 扩展组分 'pyridina/pyridina' are senior to the 扩展组分 'pyridina/silina']
(2)
1(4)-cinnolina-7(4)-pyridina-3,5(1,4)-dibenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
is senior to
1(3)-quinolina-7(4)-pyridina-3,5(1,4)-dibenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
[the 扩展组分 'cinnolina/pyridina' are senior to the 扩展组分 'quinolina/pyridina']
(3)
1(4)-pyridina-7(2)-呋喃a-3,5(1,4)-dibenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
is senior to
1(4)-pyridina-7(2)-thiophena-3,5(1,4)-dibenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
[the 扩展组分 'pyridina/呋喃a' are senior to 'pyridina/thiophena']
P-44.2.2.2.6.3 The senior linear phane system has the maximum number of skeletal nodes [criterion (c) in P-44.2.2.2.6]
Example:
1,10(1),3,5,7(1,4)-pentabenzenadecaphane (PIN)
is senior to
1,9(1),3,5,7(1,4)-pentabenzenanonaphane (PIN)
[the decaphane has more nodes than the nonaphane]
P-44.2.2.2.6.4 The senior linear phane system has the lower superatom locant(s) for the senior 扩展组分 [criterion
(d) in P-44.2.2.2.6].


### 第 396 页

Example:
1(4),7(2,5)-dipyridina-3,5(1,4),10(1)-tribenzenadecaphane (PIN)
is senior to
1(4),8(2,5)-dipyridina-3,5(1,4),10(1)-tribenzenadecaphane (PIN)
[the locant set '1,7' for the pyridina 扩展组分 is lower than the set '1,8']
P-44.2.2.2.6.5 The senior linear phane system has the lower superatom locant(s) for all 扩展组分 as a set when
compared term by term in order of increasing seniority [criterion (e) in P-44.2.2.2.6].
Example:
1,11(1),3,5,7(1,4)-pentabenzenaundecaphane (PIN)
is senior to
1,11(1),3,5,9(1,4)-pentabenzenaundecaphane (PIN)
[the locant set '1,3,5,7,11' for the 扩展组分 is lower than the set '1,3,5,9,11']
P-44.2.2.2.6.6 The senior linear phane system has the lower superatom locant(s) for all 扩展组分 when compared in
order of citation in the name [criterion (f) in P-44.2.2.2.6].
Example:
1(4)-pyridina-7(2,5)-呋喃a-9(2,5)-thiophena-3,5(1,4),11(1)-tribenzenaundecaphane (PIN)
is senior to
1(4)-pyridina-9(2,5)-呋喃a-7(2,5)-thiophena-3,5(1,4),11(1)-tribenzenaundecaphane (PIN)
[the locant set for the 扩展组分 in order of occurrence in
the name '1,7,9,3,5,11' is lower than the locant set '1,9,7,3,5,11']
P-44.2.2.2.6.7 The senior linear phane system has the lower set of attachment locants considered as a set when
compared term by term in order of increasing numerical value [criterion (g) in
P-44.2.2.2.6].
Example:
1,7(1),3,5(1,3)-tetrabenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
is senior to
1,7(1),3(1,3),5(1,4)-tetrabenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
[the locant set '1,1,1,1,3,3' for 扩展组分 attachment locants in increasing numerical order is lower than '1,1,1,1,3,4']
P-44.2.2.2.6.8 The senior linear phane system has the lower set of attachment locants when compared term by term in
their order of citation in the name [criterion (h) in P-44.2.2.2.6].


### 第 397 页

Example:
1(4)-pyridina-3(1,3),5(1,4),7(1)-tribenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
is senior to
1(4)-pyridina-3(1,4),5(1,3),7(1)-tribenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
[the locant set '4,1,3,1,4,1' for the 扩展组分 locants in order of their citation in the name is lower than '4,1,4,1,3,1']
P-44.2.2.2.6.9 The senior linear phane system has the greater number of 杂原子 introduced by skeletal
replacement ('a') nomenclature without regard to kind [criterion (i) in P-44.2.2.2.6].
Example:
6-oxa-2-thia-1(4)-pyridina-3,5(1,4),7(1)-tribenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
is senior to
2-thia-1(4)-pyridina-3,5(1,4),7(1)-tribenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
[two 杂原子 intoduced by 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature are senior to one such 杂原子]
P-44.2.2.2.6.10 The senior linear phane system has the greater number of 杂原子 introduced by skeletal
replacement ('a') nomenclature first cited in the following order: F > Cl > Br > I > O > S > Se > Te > N > P > As > Sb
> Bi > Si > Ge > Sn > Pb > B > Al > Ga > In > Tl [criterion (j) in P-44.2.2.2.6]
Example:
2,10-dioxa-1,11(4)-dipyridina-3,5,9(1,4)-tribenzenaundecaphane (PIN)
is senior to
2-oxa-10-thia-1,11(4)-dipyridina-3,5,9(1,4)-tribenzenaundecaphane (PIN)
[two oxygen atoms are senior to one oxygen atom and one sulfur atom]
P-44.2.2.2.7 Seniority criteria for 环组合体 are based on the appropriate criteria for rings and ring systems given
in P-44.2.1. Phane nomenclature is used when two 环组合体 are linked together by atoms or chains to generate a
system with at least seven nodes and two terminal rings or ring systems.
Example:
4-oxa-1(2),2,3(2,6)-tripyridina-5,6(1,4),7(1)-tribenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
16-([11,21:24,31]ter苯基]-14-yloxy)-12,22:26,32-ter吡啶;
[criteria for 环蕃命名法 (see P-52.2.5.1) are met for the PIN]
The following rules for seniority of 环组合体 are applied successively until there are no alternatives remaining:
(a) rings containing any 杂原子;
(b) 含氮的环；
(c) in the absence of nitrogen, rings containing at least one 杂原子 first cited in the following sequence; F > Cl
> Br > I > O > S > Se > Te > P > As > Sb > Bi > Si > Ge > Sn > Pb > B > Al > Ga > In > Tl;
(d) 更大的环数；


### 第 398 页

(e) 更大的原子数；
(f) the greater number of 杂原子 of any kind;
(g) the greater number of 杂原子 first cited in the following sequence: F > Cl > Br > I > O > S > Se >Te > N
> P > As > Sb > Bi > Si > Ge > Sn > Pb > B > Al > Ga > In > Tl.
Further criteria applicable to 环组合体 are found in P-44.4.
P-44.2.2.2.7.1 The senior 环组合体 is composed of rings containing any 杂原子 [criterion (a) in P-44.2.2.2.7].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
2,2′-biphosphinine (PIN) 11,21:24,31-ter苯基 (PIN)
1,1′:4′,1′′-ter苯基
P-44.2.2.2.7.2 The senior 环组合体 is composed of rings containing nitrogen [criterion (b) in P-44.2.2.2.7].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
2,2′-bi吡啶 (PIN) 2H,2′H-2,2′-bi吡喃 (PIN)
P-44.2.2.2.7.3 The senior 环组合体 in the absence of nitrogen is composed of rings containing at least one hetero
atom first cited in the following sequence: F > Cl > Br > I > O > S > Se >Te > P > As > Sb > Bi > Si > Ge > Sn > Pb >
B > Al > Ga > In > Tl [criterion (c) in P-44.2.2.2.7].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
2H,2′H-2,2′-bi吡喃 (PIN) 2H,2′H-2,2′-bithio吡喃 (PIN)
[one oxygen atom per ring is preferred to one sulfur atom per ring]
P-44.2.2.2.7.4 The senior 环组合体 is composed of the greater number of rings [criterion (d) in P-44.2.2.2.7].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
2,2′-bi喹啉 (PIN) 12,22:25,32-ter吡啶 (PIN)
2,2′:5′,2′′-ter吡啶
[a ring system consisting of two rings is senior to a ring system consisting of one ring]
P-44.2.2.2.7.5 The senior 环组合体 is composed of the greater number of atoms [criterion (e) in P-44.2.2.2.7.]
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
1H,1′H-2,2′-bi氮杂卓 (PIN) 2,4′-bi吡啶 (PIN)
[seven atoms per ring is preferred to six atoms per ring]
P-44.2.2.2.7.6 The senior 环组合体 is composed of the greater number of 杂原子 of any kind [criterion (f) in
P-44.2.2.2.7].


### 第 399 页

Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
3,3′-bipyridazine (PIN) 2,3′-bi吡啶 (PIN)
[two 杂原子 per ring is preferred to one 杂原子 per ring]
P-44.2.2.2.7.7 The senior 环组合体 is composed of the greater number of 杂原子 in the order: F > Cl > Br > I
> O > S > Se > Te > N > P > As > Sb > Bi > Si > Ge > Sn > Pb > B > Al > Ga > In > Tl [criterion(g)in P-44.2.2.2.7].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
3,3′-bi吡喃o[3,2-e][1,4]dioxepine (PIN) 2,2′-bi-3,1,5-benzoxadiarsepine (PIN)
[three oxygen atoms per ring preferred to one oxygen atom per ring]

P-44.3 SENIORITY OF ACYCLIC CHAINS (THE PRINCIPAL CHAIN)
In an acyclic compound consisting of individual atoms, alike or different (an acyclic chain), the chain on which the
nomenclature and numbering is based is called the 'principal chain'. When there is a choice for the principal chain, the
following criteria are applied, in the order listed, until a decision is reached.
The principal chain:
(a) contains the greater number of 杂原子 of any kind;
(b) has the greater number of skeletal atoms;
(c) contains the greater number of the most senior acyclic 杂原子 in the following sequence: O > S > Se > Te
> N > P > As > Sb > Bi > Si > Ge > Sn > Pb > B > Al > Ga > In >Tl.
这些标准的每一个在下面的P-44.3.1至P-44.3.3中说明。
适用于无环链的进一步标准见P-44.4。
P-44.3.1 The principal chain contains the greater number of acyclic 杂原子 of any kind [criterion (a) in P-44.3].
Examples:
(1)
2,5,8,11,14-pentaoxahexadecane (PIN)
is senior to
3,6,9,12-tetraoxaheptadecane (PIN)
[five 杂原子 are greater than four]
(2)
2,5,8,11,14-pentasilapentadecane (PIN)
is senior to
3,12-dioxa-6,9-dithiaheptadecane (PIN)
[five 杂原子 are greater than four]
P-44.3.2 主链具有更多的骨架原子[P-44.3中的标准(b)]。（已完成）


### 第 400 页

In acyclic 母体结构 the order of seniority between unsaturation and length of chain given in earlier
recommendations is reversed. Thus, the first criterion to be considered in choosing a preferred 母体 acyclic chain
is the length of the chain; unsaturation is now the second criterion.
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):

>
pentane (PIN) butane (PIN)
[five skeletal atoms is greater than four]

>
penta硅烷 (preselected tetra硅烷 (preselected
name; see P-12.2) name; see P-12.2)
[five skeletal atoms is greater than four)]

>

[eight skeletal atoms is greater than seven]

trideca-1,3-diene (PIN)
is senior to
octa-1,3,5,6-tetraene (PIN)
[thirteen skeletal atoms is greater than eight]

3,6,9,12-tetraoxapentadecane (PIN)
is senior to
3,6,9,12-tetraoxatetradecane (PIN)
[four 杂原子 in each chain and fifteen skeletal atoms is greater than fourteen]
(6)
2,5,8,11-tetrasilatridecane (PIN)
is senior to
tetra硅烷 (预选名称; see P-12.2)
[four 杂原子 in each chain and thirteen skeletal atoms is greater than four]
(7)
2,5,8,11,14-pentasilapentadecane (PIN)
is senior to
SiH -O-SiH -O-SiH
3 2 3
trisil氧杂环己烷 (预选名称; see P-12.2)
[five 杂原子 in each chain; and fifteen skeletal atoms greater than five]
P-44.3.3 The principal chain contains the greater number of the most senior acyclic 杂原子 in the order: O > S > Se
> Te > N > P > As > Sb > Bi > Si > Ge > Sn > Pb > B > Al > Ga > In >Tl [criterion (c) in P-44.3].

2,5,8,11-tetraoxatridecane (PIN)
is senior to
2,5,8-trioxa-11-thiatridecane (PIN)

P-44.4 SENIORITY CRITERIA APPLICABLE TO RINGS, RING SYSTEMS, OR ACYCLIC CHAINS
P-44.4.1 If the criteria of P-44.1 through P-44.3, where applicable, do not effect a choice of a senior 母体结构,
the following criteria are applied successively until there are no alternatives remaining. These criteria are illustrated in
P-44.4.1.1 through P-44.4.1.12.
The senior ring, ring system, or principal chain:
(a) has the greater number of 多重键 (P-44.4.1.1);
(b) has the greater number of 双键 (P-44.4.1.2);
(c) has one or more atoms with nonstandard bonding numbers (P-44.4.1.3);
(d) has the lower locants for 指示氢 (P-44.4.1.4);
(e) has the lower locants for 杂原子 introduced by 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature as a set
(P-44.4.1.5);
(f) has lower locants for 杂原子 introduced by 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature in the order: F > Cl >
Br > I > O > S > Se > Te > N > P > As > Sb > Bi > Si > Ge > Sn > Pb > B > Al > Ga > In > Tl (P-44.4.1.6);
(g) has the lower locant(s) for carbon atoms at fusion sites (P-44.4.1.7);
(h) has the lower locant for an attached group expressed as a suffix (P-44.4.1.8);
(i) has the lower locants for points of attachment (if a 取代基团) (P-44.4.1.9);
(j) has the lower locant(s) for endings or prefixes that express changes in the level of 氢化, i.e., for 'ene'
and 'yne' endings and 'hydro/dehydro' prefixes (P-44.4.1.10);
(k) has one or more isotopically modified atoms (P-44.4.1.11);
(l) has one or more stereogenic centers (P-44.4.1.12).
P-44.4.1.1 The senior ring, ring system or principal chain has the greater number of 多重键; for the purpose of
this criterion, 完全共轭 rings or ring systems are considered as consisting of noncumulative 双键 [criterion (a)
in P-44.4.1].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
(1)
> >
苯 (PIN) cyclohexene (PIN) cyclohexane (PIN)
[two 双键 greater than one 双键 that is greater than zero]


### 第 402 页

(2) >
pent-3-en-1-yne (PIN) pent-2-ene (PIN)
[two 多重键 greater than one]
(3)
3,6,9,12-tetraoxatetradec-1-ene (PIN)
is senior to
3,6,9,12-tetraoxatetradecane (PIN)
[one 双键 greater than zero]
(4)
1,10(1),4,7(1,4)-tetrabenzenadecaphan-2-en-8-yne (PIN)
is senior to
1,10(1),4,7(1,4)-tetrabenzenadecaphan-2-ene (PIN)
[two 多重键 senior to one 多重键]
or to
1,10(1),4,7(1,4)-tetrabenzenadecaphan-2-yne (PIN)
[one 双键 senior to one 三键]
P-44.4.1.2 The senior ring, ring system, or principal chain has the greater number of 双键 [criterion (b) in
P-44.4.1].
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
(1)
>
cycloicosene (PIN) cycloicosyne (PIN)
[one 双键 is senior to one 三键]
(2)
>
cycloicosa-1,8-diene (PIN) cycloicos-1-en-3-yne (PIN)
[two 双键 are senior to one 双键 and one 三键]
(3)
>
1,4-dihydro萘 (PIN) 1,2,3,4-tetrahydro萘 (PIN)
[four 双键 are greater than three]


### 第 403 页

(4)
>
1,2,5,6-tetrasilacyclooct-3-en-7-yne (PIN) 1,2,5,6-tetrasilacycloocta-3,7-diyne (PIN)
[one 双键 and one 三键 is senior to two 三键
since a 双键 is senior to a 三键]

>
hepta-1,6-diene (PIN) hept-1-en-6-yne (PIN)
[two 双键 are senior to one 双键 and one 三键
since a 双键 is senior to a 三键]
(6)
1,10(1),4,7(1,4)-tetrabenzenadecaphane-2,8-diene (PIN)
is senior to
1,10(1),4,7(1,4)-tetrabenzenadecaphan-2-en-8-yne (PIN)

one 三键 since a 双键 is senior to a 三键]
P-44.4.1.3 The senior ring, ring system, or principal chain has one or more atoms with nonstandard bonding numbers
[criterion (c) in P-44.4.1]. When choices are possible the following criteria are used in turn until a decision is reached.
P-44.4.1.3.1 When a choice is needed between two chains or between two rings or ring systems having skeletal atoms
with nonstandard bonding numbers, the one having the maximum number of atoms with nonstandard bonding numbers
is chosen as principal chain or senior ring or ring system. If a further choice is needed between the same skeletal atom
with different nonstandard bonding numbers, preference for the senior 母体结构 is given in order of the
decreasing numerical value of the bonding number, i.e., λ6 is senior to λ4.
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
(1)
>
1,3λ6-oxa硫杂环戊烷 (PIN) 1,3λ4-oxa硫杂环戊烷 (PIN)
[the nonstandard bonding number λ6 is senior to λ4]
(2)
>
1λ4,3λ5-thia磷杂环戊烷 (PIN) 1λ4,3-thia磷杂环戊烷 (PIN)
[two nonstandard bonding atoms greater than one]
(3) >

P-44.4.1.10 The senior ring, ring system or principal chain has the 最低位次 for endings or prefixes that express
changes in the degree of 氢化, i.e., for 'ene' and 'yne' endings and 'hydro/dehydro' prefixes [criterion (j) in
P-44.4.1].
The prefixes hydro or dehydro are introduced in names by an additive or subtractive operation; thus, they are not
included in the category of alphabetizable 可分离前缀 describing substitution (P-15.1.3). In names, they
occupy a place between 不可分离前缀 and the alphabetizable 可分离前缀. The prefixes hydro or
dehydro express modifications of the degree of 氢化 of a ring or ring system having the maximum number
of noncumulative 双键 (a 完全共轭 structure) and are treated for numbering like the endings 'ene' and
'yne', which fulfill the same function. In names, the prefix 'dehydro' precedes the prefix 'hydro', when both are
present. 简单的数值术语，如 'di-','tetra-', etc., are 与...一起使用 'hydro' and 'dehydro'.

Criteria for choosing 优选IUPAC名称 based on a senior 母体结构 as described in
P-44 are found in P-45.
P-44.4.1.11 The senior ring, ring system, or principal chain that has one or more isotopically modified atoms [criterion
(k) in P-44.4.1].
When there is a choice for the senior 母体结构 between isotopically modified and isotopically unmodified
compounds or between isotopically modified compounds (see Chapter P-8), the senior 母体结构 is chosen
according to the following criteria, applied successively until a decision can be made. In structures and names, nuclide
symbols enclosed in 母体heses describe isotopic substitution; nuclide symbols enclosed in square brackets describe
isotopic labeling (see Chapter P-8).
P-44.4.1.11.1 The senior 母体结构 contains the greater number of isotopically modified atoms or groups.
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
(1-2H )pentane (PIN) pentane (PIN)
1
[one isotopically modified atom is senior to none]
>
(1,1-2H )cyclohexane (PIN) (14C )cyclohexane (PIN)
2 1
[two 2H atoms are senior to one 14C]
P-44.4.1.11.2 The senior 母体结构 has the greater number of nuclides of higher atomic number for modified
atoms or groups.


### 第 411 页

Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
(14C )cyclopentane (PIN) (2H )cyclopentane (PIN)
1 1
[14C is senior to 2H]
>
[1-14C]pentane (PIN) [1-2H ]pentane (PIN)
1
[14C is senior to 2H]
P-44.4.1.11.3 The senior 母体结构 has the greater number of nuclides of higher mass number for modified atoms
or groups.
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
(3H )苯 (PIN) (2H )苯 (PIN)
1 1
[3H is senior to 2H]
>
[1-14C]pentane (PIN) [1-13C]pentane (PIN)
[14C is senior to 13C]
P-44.4.1.11.4 The senior 母体结构 has the lowest locant(s) for isotopically modified atoms or groups.
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
[4-2H ]pentanoic acid (PIN) [5-2H ]pentanoic acid (PIN)
1 1
[4-2H is senior to 5-2H]
>
(2-2H)吡啶 (PIN) (3-2H)吡啶 (PIN)
[2-2H is senior to 3-2H]
P-44.4.1.11.5 The senior 母体结构 has the lower locant(s) for nuclides of higher atomic number for modified
atoms or groups;
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
[4-13C,5-2H ]pentanoic acid (PIN) [5-13C,4-2H ]pentanoic acid (PIN)
1 1
[4-13C is senior to 5-13C ]
P-44.4.1.11.6 The senior 母体结构 has the lowest locant(s) for nuclides of higher mass number for modified
atoms or groups.
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
>
[4-13C,3-14C]butan-1-ol (PIN) [3-13C,4-14C]butan-1-ol (PIN)
[3-14C is senior to 4-14C]
P-44.4.1.12 The senior ring, ring system, or principal chain has one or more stereogenic centers [criterion (l) in
P-44.4.1].


### 第 412 页

P-44.4.1.12.1 'cis/trans' Isomerism, the 'E/Z' convention
When there is a choice between 母体结构 that differ only by 'Z' and 'E' configurations, the senior 母体
structure contains the greater number of 双键 with 'Z' configuration; when a further choice is required, the
senior 母体结构 has the lowest locant set for the 双键(s) with the 'Z' configuration. For the meaning of
'Z' and 'E' see Chapter P-9.
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
(1)
>
(Z)-cyclooctene (PIN) (E)-cyclooctene (PIN)
cis-cyclooctene trans-cyclooctene
['Z' is senior to 'E']
(2)
>
(2Z)-2-甲基but-2-enoic acid (PIN) (2E)-2-甲基but-2-enoic acid (PIN)
['Z' is senior to 'E']
>
(3)
(4Z)-hex-4-enenitrile (PIN) (4E)-hex-4-enenitrile (PIN)
['Z' is senior to 'E']
(4)
(4Z,6E)-octa-4,6-dienoic acid (PIN)
is senior to
(4E,6E)-octa-4,6-dienoic acid (PIN)
['Z,E' is senior to 'E,E']
(5)
(4Z,7E)-nona-4,7-dienoic acid (PIN)
is senior to
(4E,7Z)-nona-4,7-dienoic acid (PIN)
['4Z,7E' is senior to '4E,7Z']


### 第 413 页

P-44.4.1.12.2 Enantiomerism, the 'R' and 'S' stereodescriptors
When there is a choice between 母体结构 differing only by the configurations of the chirality centers, the
principal chain or the senior ring system is chosen by applying the CIP sequence rules 4 and 5, in the order: like
stereodescriptors such as 'RR', 'SS' have priority over unlike 'RS' and 'SR' ('l' has priority over 'u'), the 'r' over 's',
then 'R' over 'S'. CIP序列规则在第P-9章中描述。
Example (the symbol > means 'is senior to'):
(1)
(1R)-5′H-spiro[茚-1,2′-[1,3]噁唑] (PIN)
is senior to
(1S)-5′H-spiro[茚-1,2′-[1,3]噁唑] (PIN)
['R' is senior to 'S']
!
(2)
(5R,7R)-1,8-dioxadispiro[4.1.47.25]tridecane (PIN)
is senior to
(5R,7S)-1,8-dioxadispiro[4.1.47.25]tridecane (PIN)
['RR' is senior to 'RS']
P-45 SELECTION OF PREFERRED IUPAC NAMES
P-45.0 INTRODUCTION
Two or more names may result based on the same senior 母体结构 selected according to P-44, because of
different substitution patterns or multiple occurrences of the same senior 母体结构. A 母体结构 is defined
(P-15.1) as a 母体氢化物, for example, 苯, a functionalized 母体氢化物, for example cyclohexanol, or a
官能母体 compound, for example, 乙酸. Application of the criteria in this section will generate the
优选IUPAC名称.
P-45.1 Multiplication of identical senior 母体结构
P-45.2 Criteria related to number and location of 取代基团
P-45.3 Criteria related only to substituents with nonstandard bonding numbers, other criteria being equal
P-45.4 Criteria related only to isotopic modification of substituents
P-45.5 Criteria related to alphanumerical order of names
P-45.6 Criteria related only to configuration
P-45.1 MULTIPLICATION OF IDENTICAL SENIOR PARENT STRUCTURES
P-45.1.1 Multiplicative nomenclature is senior to 取代命名法 for generating 优选IUPAC名称 to
express multiple occurrences of identical senior 母体结构, other than alkanes, in the name of the 母体结构
(see P-51.3.1). In most cases multiplicative names are shorter than regular 取代名称s. A 优选IUPAC名称
is generated by 倍增命名法 when the following criteria for its use are met (see P-51.3.1).


### 第 414 页

(1) the linking bonds (single or multiple) between the central 取代基团 of the multiplicative group and all
subsequent structural units are identical; and
(2) 除中心倍增基团外的倍增基团对称取代；且
(3) the locants of all 取代基团 on the identical 母体结构, including suffix groups, are identical.
When these conditions are not met, 取代命名法 generates 优选IUPAC名称.
Example:
4,4′-oxybis(2-chloro苯甲酸) (PIN, multiplicative name)
but
4-(4-carboxy-2-chloro苯氧基)-2-chloro苯甲酸 (PIN, 取代名称)
P-45.1.2 When two or more 母体结构, rings, ring systems, or chains, satisfy the requirements for multiplicative
nomenclature (see P-15.3), the structure chosen as the 母体结构 to be multiplied is the more numerous.
Examples:
1,1′-[(苯基亚甲基)bis(sulfanediyl亚甲基)]di苯 (PIN)
(multiplication of three 苯 rings is not possible)
2,2′,2′′,2′′′-(ethane-1,2-diyldinitrilo)tetra乙酸
N,N′-(ethane-1,2-diyl)bis[N-(carboxy甲基)glycine]
4,4′,4′′-(ethene-1,1,2-triyl)tri苯胺 (PIN)
[not 4,4′-[2-(4-氨基苯基)ethene-1,1-diyl]di苯胺;
the PIN multiplies three 母体结构;
the second name multiplies only two]
[磷烷triyltris(亚甲基)]tris(phosphonic acid) (PIN)


### 第 415 页

1,1′,1′′-({[(di苯基甲基)硫烷基]di苯基甲氧基}methanetriyl)tri苯 (PIN)
(not 1,1′-({[di苯基(tri苯基甲氧基)甲基]硫烷基}亚甲基)di苯;
the PIN multiplies three 母体结构;
the second name multiplies only two)
P-45.2 CRITERIA RELATED TO NUMBER AND LOCATION OF SUBSTITUENT GROUPS
The following criteria are applied, in turn, until a decision is reached. The 优选IUPAC名称 is the name based on
the senior 母体结构 that has:
P-45.2.1 (已完成) the maximum number of 取代基团 cited as prefixes;
P-45.2.2 the lower locant set for 取代基团 cited as prefixes;
P-45.2.3 the lower locant set for 取代基团 in order of citation in the name
P-45.2.1 (已完成) The 优选IUPAC名称 is based on the senior 母体结构 that has the maximum number of
substituents cited as prefixes (other than 'hydro/dehydro') to the 母体结构.
Examples:
(1)
4-甲氧基-N-苯基苯胺 (PIN)
[not N-(4-甲氧基苯基)苯胺;
the PIN 母体结构 has more substituents,
two 简单取代基 vs. one compound substituent]
(2)
4-chloro-2-[(3-cyano苯基)甲基]benzonitrile (PIN)
[not 3-[(5-chloro-2-cyano苯基)甲基]benzonitrile;
the PIN 母体结构 has more substituents,
two (one simple and one complex) substituents vs. one complex substituent]
(3)
1-甲基-4-(苯氧基甲基)苯 (PIN)
[not [(4-甲基苯基)甲氧基]苯;
the PIN 母体结构 has more substituents;
two (one simple and one compound) substituent vs. one complex substituent]
(4)

P-45.3 CRITERIA RELATED ONLY TO SUBSTITUENTS WITH NONSTANDARD BONDING NUMBERS,
OTHER CRITERIA BEING EQUAL
The following criteria are relative only to substituents with nonstandard bonding numbers applied in turn, until a
decision is reached, earlier criteria having been satisfied.
P-45.3.1 the maximum number of 取代基团(s) with the higher bonding number cited as prefixes;
P-45.3.2 the lower locant set for 取代基团(s) with the higher bonding number cited as prefixes.


### 第 423 页

P-45.3.1 The 优选IUPAC名称 is based on the senior 母体结构 that has the maximum number of
取代基团(s) with the higher bonding number cited as prefixes and directly connected to the 母体结构.
Examples:
3-(λ5-磷烷基)-2-(磷烷基甲基)propanoic acid (PIN)
[not 3-磷烷基-2-(λ5-磷烷基甲基)propanoic acid;
the PIN 母体结构 contains the 取代基团 having the highest bonding number, 'λ5' > 'λ3']
4-(2λ5-diphosphan-1-yl)-2-(2-di磷烷基乙基)butanenitrile (PIN)
[not 4-di磷烷基-2-[2-(2λ5-diphosphan-1-yl)乙基]butanenitrile;
the PIN 母体结构 contains the 取代基团 having the highest bonding number, 'λ5' > 'λ3']
3-(λ6-硫烷基)-2-(λ4-硫烷基甲基)propanoic acid (PIN)
[not 3-(λ4-硫烷基)-2-(λ6-硫烷基甲基)propanoic acid;
the PIN 母体结构 contains the 取代基团 having the highest bonding number, 'λ6' > 'λ4']
P-45.3.2 The 优选IUPAC名称 has the lower locant set for 取代基团(s) with the higher bonding
number(s) cited as prefixes.
Example:
5-(1λ5-diphospan-1-yl)-2-[3-(2λ5-diphospan-1-yl)丙基]pentanoic acid (PIN)
[not 5-(2λ5-diphospan-1-yl)-2-[3-(1λ5-diphospan-1-yl)丙基]pentanoic acid]
The locant '1λ5' for the nonstandard bonding number directly bonded to the 母体结构 is lower than '2λ5'.
P-45.4 CRITERIA RELATED ONLY TO ISOTOPIC MODIFICATION OF SUBSTITUENTS
The following criteria are relative only to substituents with isotopic modification applied in turn, until a decision is
reached, earlier criteria having been satisfied.
P-45.4.1 The 优选IUPAC名称 is based on the senior 母体结构 that has the lowest locant(s) for isotopically
modified 取代基团.
Example:
2-bromo-1-{[2-(81Br)bromo戊基]oxy}pentane (PIN)
[not 2-(81Br)bromo-1-[(2-bromo戊基)oxy]pentane]
The isotopically modified substituent for the PIN is attached at '1' which is lower than '2'.
P-45.4.2 The 优选IUPAC名称 is based on the senior 母体结构 that has the lowest locant(s) for nuclides of
higher atomic number.
Example:
2-(13C)甲氧基-N-[2-[(18O)甲氧基乙基]ethan-1-amine (PIN)
[not 2-(18O)甲氧基-N-[2-(13C)甲氧基乙基]ethan-1-amine]
18O > 13C; the 18O isotopically modified substituent for the PIN is attached at 'N' which is lower than '2'.
P-45.4.3 The 优选IUPAC名称 is based on the senior 母体结构 that has the lowest locant(s) for nuclides of
higher mass number.


### 第 424 页

Example:
4-[2-13C]乙基-2-(2-[2-14C]乙基戊基)heptan-1-ol (PIN)
[not 4-[2-14C]乙基-2-(2-[2-13C]乙基戊基)heptan-1-ol
14C > 13C; the 14C isotopically modified substituent for the PIN is attached at '2' which is lower than '4'
P-45.5 CRITERIA RELATED TO ALPHANUMERICAL ORDER OF NAMES
The 优选IUPAC名称 is the name that is earlier in alphanumerical order (see P-14.5). Alphabetic letters are
considered first in the order that they appear in the name; all Roman letters are considered before any italic letters,
unless the latter are used as locants or are a part of a compound or 组合位次, for example, 'N' and '4a'. Then, if
still there is a choice, numerical locants are considered in the order of their appearance in the name.
Examples:
(1)
1-bromo-4-chloro-2-{2-[(1,4-dibromonaphthalen-2-yl)甲氧基]乙基}萘 (PIN)
[not 1,4-dibromo-2-{[2-(1-bromo-4-chloronaphthalen-2-yl)ethoxy]甲基}萘;
in!both!names!the!locant!set!is!'1,2,4',!and!the!locants!appear!in!the!name!in!the!same!order!set!'1,4,2'!so!no!!
decision!can!be!made!by!P#45.2.2!or!P#45.2.3;!but!'bromo' in the PIN is earlier alphabetically than 'dibromo']
(2)
2-bromo-4-chloro-N-(2,4-dibromo苯基)苯胺 (PIN)
[not 2,4-dibromo-N-(2-bromo-4-chloro苯基)苯胺;
'bromo' in the PIN is earlier alphabetically than 'dibromo']
(3)
13-bromo-14-chloro-2-(3,4-dibromonaphthalen-2-yl)-1(2)-naphthalena-3,5(1,4),7(1)-tribenzenaheptaphane (PIN)
[not 13,14-dibromo-2-(3-bromo-4-chloronaphthalen-2-yl)-1(2)-naphthalena-3,5(1,4),7(1)-tribenzenaheptaphane;
'bromo' in the PIN is earlier alphabetically than 'dibromo'.]
(4)
4-(1,2-difluoro丙基)-5,6-dinitroheptanoic acid (PIN)
[not 4-(1,2-dinitro丙基)-5,6-difluoroheptanoic acid;
'difluoro' in the PIN is earlier alphabetically than 'dinitro']


### 第 425 页

(5)
5-(81Br)bromo-3-[3-(81Br)bromobutan-2-yl]-4-nitrohexanoic acid (PIN)
[not 5-(81Br)bromo-3-[2-(81Br)bromo-1-nitro丙基]-4-甲基hexanoic acid;
'bromo-bromo-butanyl' is lower alphanumerically than 'bromo-bromo-nitro']
[Note: The 'B' of the element symbol 'Br' is not a factor in the alphabetization]
P-45.6 CRITERIA RELATED ONLY TO CONFIGURATION
P-45.6.1 Introduction
This section is concerned only with the main principles for specification of configuration in names of organic
compounds. The spatial structure of an organic compound is systematically indicated by one or more affixes added to a
name that does not itself prescribe configuration; such affixes are generally called 'stereodescriptors' and do not change
优选IUPAC名称 of a compound established by the principles of nomenclature described in other sections of
these recommendations (see P-44 and P-45), under the condition that no choice has yet been encountered during the
construction of 优选IUPAC名称. When there is a choice, 优选IUPAC名称 must be constructed in
conformity with the rules for choosing the principal chain or the senior ring or ring system and also with the priority of
the stereodescriptors. Thus, stereoisomers, such as enantiomers and cis/trans isomers, have names, substitutive or
multiplicative, that differ only in the stereodescriptors used. Stereodescriptors describing 优选IUPAC名称 are
all Cahn-Ingold-Prelog CIP descriptors, such as E, Z, R, S, r and s discussed and illustrated in Chapter P-9.
P-45.6.2 General principles. The choice of a 母体结构 based on the number and location of 多重键 and
双键 (see P-44.4.1.1, P-44.4.1.2, and P-44.4.1.12) is not configuration dependent. As an example, the
configuration of the 双键 may be Z or E in the following alkene that is named according to P-31.1. The names
with and without stereodescriptors are the same, i.e. butene. Preferred IUPAC names are generated from the preferred
IUPAC name of the alkene when stereodescriptors are correct.
but-2-ene (PIN)
(2Z)-but-2-ene (PIN)
(2E)-but-2-ene (PIN)
In contrast, multiplicative names (see P-45.1 and P-15.3) are configuration dependent as shown below. Two different
procedures are required: a multiplicative name to describe identical configurations ('E' or 'Z'; or 'R' or 'S') in identical
units and a 取代名称 to describe different configurations ('E' and 'Z' or 'R' and 'S') as shown by the first
example below.
Example 1:
(1E,1′E)-1,1′-sulfanediyldi(cyclooct-1-ene) (PIN)
(a multiplicative name)


### 第 426 页

(1Z,3S)-3-{2-[(1R,2E)-cyclooct-2-en-1-yl]乙基}cyclooct-1-ene (PIN)
(1-cis,3S)-3-{2-[(1R,2-trans)-cyclooct-2-en-1-yl]乙基}cyclooct-1-ene
(取代名称s; for the priority of 'Z' > 'E' > 'R' > 'S', see P-92)
Example 2:
1,1′-sulfanediylbis{4-[(2R)-butan-2-yl]苯} (PIN)
(a multiplicative name)
1-[(2R)-butan-2-yl]-4-({4-[(2S)-butan-2-yl]苯基}硫烷基)苯 (PIN)
(a 取代名称; a multiplicative name is not allowed)
[not 1-[(2S)-butan-2-yl]-4-({4-[(2R)-butan-2-yl]苯基}硫烷基)苯;
also a 取代名称 but since the alphabetic characters and locants
(ignoring the configuration symbols)
are identical the configurational symbols are compared and 'R' precedes 'S']
Example 3:
1,1′-oxybis[3,4-bis(1-chloro乙基)苯] (PIN)
4-{3,4-bis[(1R)-1-chloro乙基]苯氧基}-1,2-bis[(1S)-1-chloro乙基]苯 (PIN)
[not 4-{3,4-bis[(1S)-1-chloro乙基]苯氧基}-1,2-bis[(1R)-1-chloro乙基]苯;
'R' precedes 'S']
1,2-bis[(1S)-1-chloro乙基]-4-{3-[(1R)-1-chloro乙基]-4-[(1S)-1-chloro乙基]苯氧基}苯 (PIN)
(a 取代名称; a multiplicative name is not allowed)
[not 4-{3,4-bis[(1S)-1-chloro乙基]苯氧基}-2-[(1R)-1-chloro乙基]-1-[(1S)-1-chloro乙基]苯;
'1,2,4' is senior to '4,2,1' (see P-45.2.3)]


### 第 427 页

P-45.6.3 When names based on alphanumerical order and isotopic descriptors are the same, further choice depends on
the alphabetic order of the stereochemical descriptors 'R' and 'S'.
Example:
1-[(1R)-1-bromo乙基]-1-[(1S)-1-bromo乙基]cyclopentane (PIN)
[not 1-[(1S)-1-bromo乙基]-1-[(1R)-1-bromo乙基]cyclopentane;
'R' precedes 'S']
P-46 THE PRINCIPAL CHAIN IN SUBSTITUENT GROUPS
P-46.0 Introduction
P-46.1 The principal substituent chain
P-46.2 Principal substituent chains in isotopically labeled compounds
P-46.3 Principal substituent chains in compounds with stereogenic centers
P-46.0 INTRODUCTION
Compound acyclic substituents, i.e., substituted acyclic substituents, consist of a principal chain and one or more
acyclic substituents. If the substituent to the principal chain also has (an) acyclic substituent(s), it itself is a compound
substituent; the resulting complete substituent is called a complex acyclic substituent. Complex substituents are named
by extending the methods given below for compound substituents.
复合取代基通过两种方式命名：
(1) 使用烷基型取代基[见P-29.2(1)]；
(2) 使用烷烃基型取代基[见P-29.2(2)]。
Alkyl and alkanyl 取代基团 have been defined in Section P-29. Simple alkyl 取代基团 have their free
valence(s) denoted by the suffixes 'yl', 'ylidene' or 'ylidyne' only at position 1. Simple alkanyl 取代基团 have
their 自由价(s) denoted by the suffixes 'yl' or 'ylidene' which may be located at any position of the chain except
position 1. Both alkyl and alkanyl 取代基团 can form 复合取代基团; for example,
CH -C(CH ) - is 1,1-di甲基乙基, a compound alkyl 取代基团, by method (1); and 2-甲基propan-2-yl, a
3 3 2
compound alkanyl 取代基团, by method (2). In some cases, a 复合取代基团 resulting from
substitution of the principal chain by alkyl or alkanyl 取代基团 results in the same structure that corresponds to
a simple alkanyl 取代基团; for example, CH -CH -CH -CH -CH(CH )- is 1-甲基戊基 by method (1), but
3 2 2 2 3
hexan-2- yl by method (2).
P-46.1 THE PRINCIPAL SUBSTITUENT CHAIN
Selection of the principal chain of a compound substituent is accomplished 按照 the following criteria,
applied successively in the order given until a decision is reached. They are listed here and illustrated in P-46.1.1
through P-46.1.13. 取代基主链具有：
(a) the greater number of 杂原子; this criterion is used only in method (2) in P-46.0;
(b) 更多的骨架原子，即最长链；
In acyclic substituents the order of seniority between unsaturation and length of chain given in earlier
recommendations is reversed. Thus, the first criterion to be considered in choosing a preferred acyclic substituent is
the length of the chain; unsaturation is now a lower criterion [see (d)].
(c) the greater number of 杂原子 in the order: O > S > Se > Te > N > P > As > Sb > Bi > Si > Ge > Sn > Pb >
B > Al > Ga > In > Tl;
(d) the greater number of 多重键 regardless of type, then the greater number of 双键;
(e) 一个或多个具有非标准键合数的原子；
(f) the 最低位次 for 杂原子; this criterion is used only in method (2) in P-46.0;
(g) the 最低位次 for 杂原子 appearing first in the order: O > S > Se > Te > N > P > As > Sb > Bi > Si >
Ge > Sn > Pb > B > Al > Ga > In > Tl;
(h) the 最低位次 for 自由价 of any kind ('yl', 'ylidene', 'ylidyne');
(i) the lowest locant(s) for 多重键, regardless of type, then the lower locant(s) for 双键;
(j) 对具有非标准键合数的原子最低的位次；
(k) 最大数量的任何种类的取代基；此标准适用于P-46.0中的方法(1)和(2)两者；


### 第 428 页

(l) the 最低位次 for substituents; this criterion is applicable to both methods (1) and (2) in P-46.0;
(m) the 最低位次 for the substituent(s) cited earlier in alphanumerical order; this criterion is applicable to both
methods (1) and (2) in P-46.0.
P-46.1.1 The principal substituent chain has the greater number of 杂原子 [criterion (a) in P-46.1]; this criterion is
used only in method (2) of P-46.0.
Example:
7-丙基-2,4,6,8-tetrasilanonan-1-yl (优选前缀)
[not 7-(甲基甲硅烷基)-2,4,6-trisiladecan-1-yl]
P-46.1.2 The principal substituent chain has the greater number of skeletal atoms, i.e., the longest chain [criterion (b) in
P-46.1]. This criterion is applicable to both methods (1) and (2) of P-46.0; both methods generate simple and compound
取代基团.
Examples:
(2) pentan-2-ylidene (优选前缀) (1) 1-甲基丁基idene

(a 简单取代基 group) (a 复合取代基团)

(a 复合取代基团) (a 复合取代基团)
P-46.1.3 The principal substituent chain has the greater number of 杂原子 in the order: O > S > Se > Te > N > P >
As > Sb > Bi > Si > Ge > Sn > Pb > B > Al > Ga > In > Tl [criterion (c) in P-46.1].
Examples:
1-(甲硅烷基硫烷基)二甲硅醚基 (预选前缀)
[not 1-(甲硅烷基oxy)disilathianyl; 'O' > 'S']

[not 5-[2-(甲基甲硅烷基)ethoxy]-2-oxa-4,6,8-trisiladecan-1-yl;
two oxygens are senior to one oxygen]
P-46.1.4 The principal chain has the greater number of 多重键 regardless of type, then the greater number of
双键 [criterion (d) in P-46.1]; this criterion is applicable to methods (1) and (2) in P-46.0; both generate simple
and 复合取代基团.
Examples:
(2) hept-1-en-4-ylidene (优选前缀) (1) 1-丙基but-3-en-1-ylidene

### 第 429 页

(2) 4-乙基hexa-1,4-dien-3-yl (1) 1-e噻吩甲基-2-乙基but-2-en-1-yl
(优选前缀; a 复合取代基团) (a 复合取代基团)
P-46.1.5 The principal substituent chain has one or more atoms with nonstandard bonding numbers [criterion (e) in
P-46.1]. When a choice is needed between two substituent chains having skeletal atoms with nonstandard bonding
numbers, the one having the maximum number of atoms with nonstandard bonding numbers is chosen as principal
substituent chain. If a further choice is needed between the same skeletal atom with different nonstandard bonding
numbers, preference for the principal substituent chain is given in order of the decreasing numerical value of the
bonding number, i.e., λ6 is senior to λ4.
Examples:
5-{[(甲氧基甲基)硫烷基]甲基}-4,9-dioxa-2,7λ4-dithiadecan-1-yl (优选前缀)
[not 5-{[(甲氧基甲基)-λ4-硫烷基]甲基}-4,9-dioxa-2,7-dithiadecan-1-yl;
one nonstandard bonding atom vs. zero]
5-({[(甲基硫烷基)甲基]-λ6-硫烷基}甲基)-4-oxa-2λ4,7λ4,9λ4-trithiadecan-1-yl (优选前缀)
[not 5-({[(甲基-λ4-硫烷基)甲基]-λ4-硫烷基}甲基)-4-oxa-2λ4,7λ6,9- trithiadecan-1-yl;
three nonstandard bonding atoms vs. two]
5-({[(甲基-λ6-硫烷基)甲基]-λ4-硫烷基}甲基)-4-oxa-2λ4,7λ6 ,9λ6-trithiadecan-1-yl (优选前缀)
[not 5-({[(甲基-λ6-硫烷基)甲基]-λ6-硫烷基 }甲基)-4-oxa-2λ4,7λ4,9λ6-trithiadecan-1-yl;
two λ6 nonstandard bonding atoms and one λ4 nonstandard bonding atoms vs.
two λ4 and one λ6 nonstandard bonding atoms]
P-46.1.6 The principal substituent chain has the 最低位次 for 杂原子 [criterion (f) in P-46.1]; this criterion is
used only in method (2) of P-46.0.
This is a change. Heteroatoms in chains are now considered as part of the 母体氢化物; as such they have
seniority over suffixes for numbering (see P-14.4; see also P-15.4.3); this change makes 'a' terms nondetachable
in 骨架置换 in chains, rings, and ring systems.
Example:
10-(1,3,5,7-tetrasilanonan-1-yl)-2,5,7,9-tetrasilaundecan-11-yl (优选前缀)
[not 10-(1,3,5,8-tetrasilanonan-1-yl)-3,5,7,9-tetrasilaundecan-11-yl;
the locant set in the principal substituent chain '2,5,7,9' is lower than the set '3,5,7,9']
P-46.1.7 The principal substituent chain has the 最低位次 for 杂原子 appearing first in the order: O > S > Se
> Te > N > P > As > Sb > Bi > Si > Ge > Sn > Pb > B > Al > Ga > In > Tl [criterion(g) in P-46.1]; this criterion is used
only in method (2) of P-46.0.
Example:
2-{[({[(丁基甲硅烷基)甲基]甲硅烷基}甲氧基)甲基]硫烷基}-3-oxa-5-thia-7,9-disilatridecan-1-yl (优选前缀)
[not 2-{[({[(丁基甲硅烷基)甲基]甲硅烷基}甲基)硫烷基]甲氧基}-5-oxa-3-thia-7,9-disilatridecan-1-yl;
'3-oxa' is senior to '5-oxa']
P-46.1.8 The principal substituent chain has the 最低位次 for 自由价 of any kind in the order 'yl' > 'ylidene'
> 'ylidyne' [criterion (h) in P-46.1].

the λ6 nonstandard bonding atom in the PIN name is at position '7' which is lower than '9']
P-46.1.11 The principal substituent chain has the greatest number of substituents of any kind [criterion (k) in P-46.1].

取代基团, respectively.

P-50 INTRODUCTION
Many compounds can have two or more names 按照 several methods recommended by IUPAC for their
formation, one of which is recommended herein as 优选IUPAC名称 (PIN). This Chapter summarizes the
selection rules that are recommended in Chapters P-1 through P-4 for generation of 优选IUPAC名称 for
compounds described in these Chapters and also in Chapters P-6 to P-10 where applicable. Substitutive nomenclature is
the principal type of nomenclature for organic compounds; however, other types are recommended because substitutive
nomenclature was never recommended for naming certain classes of compounds or because they represent a
simplification when the 取代名称s become long and cumbersome.
In Chapter P-1, several types of nomenclature are discussed. All of them are used to generate 优选IUPAC名称
and names for 通用命名法. Functional class nomenclature (see P-51.2) is used to generate names of well
defined classes, such as acid halides and esters. Multiplicative nomenclature (see P-51.3) is used to enable several
occurrences of the same 母体结构 in a single molecule all to be expressed as 母体结构, although this is
permitted only under certain restrictive conditions. When these conditions are not fulfilled, 取代命名法 is
recommended. 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature (see P-51.4) is used to simplify 取代名称s of acyclic
compounds containing 杂原子 (usually by eliminating many nesting operations); it is mandatory for naming
饱和杂环化合物 having more than ten members and in heteropolyalicyclic nonfused bridged and spiro
ring systems.
In Chapter P-2, most of the rules are unequivocal because they generate 优选IUPAC名称 of cyclic and acyclic
compounds in themselves. When rings, ring systems, and chains are composed of entities that are themselves rings or
ring systems, interconnected or not by chains, 环蕃命名法 is recommended to generate preferred IUPAC
names; the selection of these names is discussed in P-52.2.5. The selection of 优选IUPAC名称 for ring
assemblies is treated in P-52.2.7. The selection of 优选IUPAC名称 is also necessary for 取代基团
derived from the 母体氢化物 described in Chapter P-2.
In Chapter P-3, the level of saturation expressed by the prefixes 'hydro/dehydro' or 'ene/yne' endings is considered. In
the cases of 特征基团 cited as prefixes and 官能母体化合物, the selection is between retained
names and 系统名称 as components of 优选IUPAC名称.
In Chapter P-4, the various seniority orders described are unequivocal, with the exception of substituted 母体
structures that must be analyzed as 优选IUPAC名称. In P-44, comprehensive rules for the selection of a
preferred 母体结构 are presented. A new concept for IUPAC nomenclature is described in Section P-45 called
'Selection of 优选IUPAC名称'. The selection of 优选IUPAC名称 is based on hierarchical rules based
on seniority orders for determining the one and only preferred 母体名称 based on the senior 母体结构
described in P-44. This issue is discussed in P-58.
P-51 SELECTING THE PREFERRED IUPAC TYPE OF NOMENCLATURE
P.51.0 Introduction
P-51.1 Selecting the preferred type of nomenclature
P-51.2 Functional class nomenclature (see P-15.2)
P-51.3 Multiplicative nomenclature (see P-15.3)
P-51.4 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature (see P-15.4)
P-51.5 Conjunctive nomenclature vs. 取代命名法


### 第 435 页

P-51.0 INTRODUCTION
When a choice is needed between the several types of IUPAC nomenclature, the following selection rules must be
applied. Sections P-51.1 through P-51.4 give specific rules for each type of nomenclature and examples.
P-51.1 SELECTING THE PREFERRED TYPE OF NOMENCLATURE
When there is a choice between two types of nomenclature, the preferred type of nomenclature is selected in accordance
with the following rules.
P-51.1.1 Substitutive nomenclature is preferred to functional class nomenclature, except for the classes described in
P-51.2 for which no 取代名称s are prescribed.
Example:
(CH ) C=N-N=C(CH )
3 2 3 2
丙酮 azine (see P-68.3.1.2.3)
di(propan-2-ylidene)hydrazine (PIN)
P-51.1.2 取代命名法优先于连接命名法。
Example:
2,3-萘di乙酸 (a conjunctive name)
2,2′-(萘-2,3-diyl)di乙酸 (PIN;
a 取代名称; see P-15.6.1.4)
P-51.1.3 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature is preferred to 取代命名法 when 杂原子 are
present in chains (see P-51.4.1) and the criteria for the use of skeletal nomenclature are met.
Example:
3-phospha-2,5,7-trisilaoctane (PIN, a 骨架置换 ('a') name)
(甲基甲硅烷基)({[(甲基甲硅烷基)甲基]甲硅烷基}甲基)磷烷 (a 取代名称)
P-51.1.4 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature is preferred to 倍增命名法 (see P-51.4.1) when the
criteria for use of skeletal nomenclature are met.
Example:
8-thia-2,4,6-trisiladecane (PIN, 骨架置换 ('a') name)
1-[(乙基硫烷基)甲基]-1′-甲基-1,1′-[硅烷diylbis(亚甲基)]bis(硅烷);
(a multiplicative name)
P-51.1.5 Multiplicative nomenclature (P-15.3, P-51.3), as a subset of 取代命名法, is preferred to simple
取代命名法 when the criteria for its use are met; it allows multiple occurrences of the principal
特征基团 or compound class to be treated together.
Example:
2,2′-[ethane-1,2-diylbis(oxy)]di乙酸 (PIN, multiplicative name)
[2-(carboxy甲氧基)ethoxy]乙酸 (取代名称)
P-51.2 FUNCTIONAL CLASS NOMENCLATURE
In many cases, functional class nomenclature and 取代命名法 can be used to give two names to one
compound, for example 甲基 bromide, a functional class name, and bromomethane, a 取代名称, for CH -Br.
3
Substitutive names have now replaced many functional class names, but not all. In the context of preferred IUPAC
names, it is essential to correctly use the two types of nomenclature. In P-51.2.1, the functional class names that are
优选IUPAC名称 are given. In P-51.2.2 the functional class names that 可用于通用命名法 are
discussed and exemplified; for the 取代名称s that are 优选IUPAC名称 corresponding to these
functional class names, see P-15.2.

P-51.2.2 Functional class nomenclature for 通用命名法
A certain number of classes can still be named for 通用命名法 by applying functional class nomenclature.
They are described in P-15.2. For these classes, 优选IUPAC名称 are 取代名称s.

P-51.3 MULTIPLICATIVE NOMENCLATURE (see P-15.3)
Multiplicative nomenclature is used to name assemblies of identical units linked by di- or polyvalent groups formed
according to P-15.3.2. This subsection describes the formation of preferred IUPAC multiplicative names according to
the principles and rules discussed in P-15.3. Substitutive nomenclature is used when the conditions for constructing
multiplicative names are not met. Further, 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature (see P-15.4) and 环蕃命名法
(see P-26) are used rather than 倍增命名法 when the conditions for their use are met in order to
simplify name construction when multiplicative names become complex and cumbersome.
In these recommendations, identical 母体结构 do not have to have a 主要特征基团 in order to
construct a multiplicative name, which was necessary in earlier recommendations.
P-51.3.1 Preferred IUPAC multiplicative names
For a multiplicative name to be categorized as an IUPAC preferred name, certain restrictive conditions must be met.
Multiplicative nomenclature is preferred to 取代命名法 for generating 优选IUPAC名称 to express
multiple occurrences of identical 母体结构, other than alkanes when
(1) the linking bonds (single or multiple) between the central 取代基团 of the multiplicative group and all
subsequent structural units are identical and
(2) 除中心倍增基团外的倍增基团对称取代；且
(3) the locants of all 取代基团 on the identical 母体结构, including suffix groups, are identical.
In these recommendations, all 取代基团, including the 主要特征基团s must be identical
and have the same locant in order to construct a multiplicative name. This is a change from earlier
recommendations where such locants did not need to be identical.
The first two specific conditions are related to the linking di- or polyvalent groups. They are defined and exemplified in
Section P-15.3.1.2. Simple and concatenated groups are used when the conditions expressed in P-15.3.1.2.1 and
P-15.3.1.2.2 are fulfilled.
Examples:
2,2′-sulfanediyldi乙酸 (PIN, multiplicative name)
[(carboxy甲基)硫烷基]乙酸 (取代名称)
4,4′-oxydi(cyclohexane-1-羧酸) (PIN, multiplicative name)
4-[(4-carboxy环己基)oxy]cyclohexane-1-羧酸 (取代名称)

P-51.4 SKELETAL REPLACEMENT ('a') NOMENCLATURE
骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature is used to generate 优选IUPAC名称 in place of substitutive or
multiplicative names when four or more heterounits are present in an acyclic chain (see P-51.3.1). 骨架置换
('a') nomenclature is the only recommended method for certain types of cyclic compounds.
In these recommendations, groups of atoms having a simple multivalent name are considered as a unit, hence the
term heterounits includes both 杂原子 and heterogroups. Heterogroups were not considered as a single
heterounit in previous recommendations.
P-51.4.1 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature in acyclic chains
P-51.4.1.1 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature rather than substitutive or multiplicative names must be used to
generate 优选IUPAC名称 for acyclic structures when four or more heterounits are present in a unbranched chain
containing at least one carbon atom and when none of the 杂原子 constitute all or part of the principal
特征基团 of the compound.
A heterounit is a set of 杂原子 having a name of its own such as, -SS-, disulfanediyl; -SiH -O-SiH -, disil氧杂环己烷-
2 2
1,3-diyl; -SOS-, dithi氧杂环己烷diyl (not -OSiH O- nor -OSO- that correspond to three consecutive units
2
'oxy硅烷diyloxy' and 'oxysulfanediyloxy', respectively). Acids such as 碳酸 or phosphorus, arsenic, and
antimony acids, when representing the 母体 compound or the principal group, are not considered as units. In presence
of a 特征基团 having seniority for citation as a suffix, the group -O-P(O)(OCH )-O- is composed of three
3
units (see example 11 below).
P-51.4.1.2 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature generates new 无环母体氢化物 whose numbering is fixed, as
it is for heterocyclic rings and ring systems. Suffixes, endings, and prefixes are added 按照 this fixed
numbering.
Fixed numbering for heteroacyclic 母体结构 named by 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature is a major
change to Rule C-0.6 (ref. 1) where 主要特征基团s and 自由价 were preferred over 杂原子
for 低位次.
Examples:
3-phospha-2,5,7-trisilaoctane [PIN, 骨架置换 ('a') name]
(甲基甲硅烷基)({[(甲基甲硅烷基)甲基]甲硅烷基}甲基)磷烷 (a 取代名称)
8-thia-2,4,6-trisiladecane [PIN, 骨架置换 ('a') name]
1-[(乙基硫烷基)甲基]-1′-甲基-1,1′-[硅烷diylbis(亚甲基)]bis(硅烷); (a multiplicative name)
({[(乙基硫烷基)甲基]甲硅烷基}甲基)[(甲基甲硅烷基)甲基]硅烷; (a 取代名称)
3,6,9,12-tetraoxatetradecanedioic acid [PIN, a 骨架置换 ('a') name]
2,2′-{ethane-1,2-diylbis[(oxyethane-2,1-diyl)oxy]di乙酸; (a multiplicative name)
2-(2-{2-[2-(carboxy甲氧基)ethoxy]ethoxy}ethoxy)乙酸; (a 取代名称)

P-51.4.1.3 The same number of 特征基团 that would be expressed as suffixes in 取代名称s must be
present in 骨架置换 ('a') names.
Examples:
9-imino-2,8,10,16-tetraazaheptadecanedi脒 (PIN)
(a di脒 expressed as a 主要特征基团 is senior to a carbonimidic diamide)
16-氨基-N-(14-氨基-3,6,9,12-tetraazatetradecan-1-yl)-2,5,8,11,14-pentaazahexadecanamide [PIN;
an amide expressed as a 主要特征基团 is senior to
尿素, a carbonic diamide, or an amine expressed as a 主要特征基团;
since four 杂原子 are also present in the N-取代基团,
it must also be named by 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature]


### 第 443 页

13-氨基-N-(2-{[2-({2-[(2-氨基乙基)氨基]乙基}氨基)乙基]氨基}乙基)-2,5,8,11-tetraazatridecanamide (PIN;
an amide expressed as 特征基团 is senior to 尿素, a carbonic diamide,
and to an amine expressed as a 特征基团;
since only three 杂原子 are present in the N-取代基团, it must be named substitutively)
甲基 7,14,21,28-tetraacetamido-2,9,16,23-tetraoxo-3,10,17,24- tetraazatriacontan-30-oate (PIN;
an ester is senior to an amide or a ketone)
P-51.4.1.4 The chain must be terminated by C or one of the following 杂原子: P, As, Sb, Bi, Si, Ge, Sn, Pb, B, Al,
Ga, In, or Tl.
In these recommendations heterochains may be terminated by certain 杂原子 and not just by carbon atoms.
Previous recommendations required a heterochain to be terminated by carbon atoms.
Example:
2-oxa-4-thia-1,5-disilapentane (PIN)
P-51.4.2 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature for cyclic compounds
骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature is the only recommended method to generate names for certain heterocyclic
compounds.
P-51.4.2.1 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature is used to derive 优选IUPAC名称 for heteromonocyclic
compounds having more than ten ring atoms (see P-22.2.3).
骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature is also possible with rings having less than 10 ring members if the skeletal
replacement ('a') prefix represents a metal as defined in P-69.4.
Adapting the principles of the Hantzsch-Widman系统 to the elements of Groups 1-12 in and including their
骨架置换 ('a') prefixes would be 一个重大改变 from previous recommendations even though the names
of such 有机金属 compounds involving these elements are not PINs at this time.
Examples:
1-azacyclododeca-1,3,5,7,9,11-hexaene (PIN)
1,1-dichloro-2,3,4,5-tetra甲基platinole (Hantzsch-Widman name)
1,1-dichloro-2,3,4,5-tetra甲基-1-platinacyclopenta-2,4-diene
(a 骨架置换 name; see P-69.4)
The option to include the metallic elements in addition to the metals in Groups 13 through 16 in the Hantzsch-
Widman system and their 骨架置换 ('a') prefixes (see P-69.4) is 一个重大改变 from previous
recommendations for the Hantzsch-Widman系统.


### 第 444 页

P-51.4.2.2 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature is used to derive 优选IUPAC名称 for heterocyclic von
Baeyer ring systems (see P-23.3.1).
Example:
2,6-dioxabicyclo[3.3.2]decane (PIN)
P-51.4.2.3 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature is used to derive 优选IUPAC名称 for heterocyclic spiro
母体氢化物 consisting of two or more 饱和 monocyclic rings (see P-24.2.4.1.1)
Example:
7-aza-1-thiaspiro[4.5]decane (PIN)
P-51.4.2.4 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature is used to derive 优选IUPAC名称 for heteropolycyclic ring
systems for which fusion nomenclature based on heterocyclic 母体 rings is not applicable (see P-25.5.1).
Example:
1,3a1,4-triaza亚苯基 (PIN)
[not 1,4,9b-triaza亚苯基; see P-25.3.3.3]
P-51.4.2.5 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature is used to derive 优选IUPAC名称 for heterophane ring
systems (see P-26.5) and for heterofullerenes (see P-27.5).
P-51.4.2.6 When necessary, the choice of 优选IUPAC名称 of a heterocyclic 母体结构 is made before the
insertion of 骨架置换 ('a') prefixes. This is the case with assemblies of identical heterocyclic compounds
(see P-28.4) composed of heterocyclic compounds of the von Baeyer type and with monocyclic compounds having
more than ten members.
Examples:
13,23,34-trioxa-11,21:26,31-tercycloundecane (PIN; see P-28.3.1)
3,3′,4′′-trioxa-1,1′:6′,1′′-tercycloundecane
(not 13,23:29,34-ter-1-oxacycloundecane)
23-thia-12,22:26,32-terbicyclo[2.2.1]heptane (PIN; see P-28.3.1)
{not 2,6-bis(bicyclo[2.2.1]heptan-2-yl)-3-thiabicyclo[2.2.1]heptane}
3′-thia-2,2′:6′,2′′-terbicyclo[2.2.1]heptane
P-51.4.2.7 The choice between two or more acceptable names may depend on the type of nomenclature used. This is
the case with un饱和杂单环化合物 for which three names are acceptable, as discussed in P-52.2.3.


### 第 445 页

Example:
1-azacyclotrideca-2,4,6,8,10,12-hexaene [PIN, a 骨架置换 ('a') name]
1-azacyclotridecine [a skeletal relacement ('a') name for use in fusion nomenclature]
1H-1-aza[13]轮烯
P-51.5 CONJUNCTIVE NOMENCLATURE vs. SUBSTITUTIVE NOMENCLATURE
When there is a choice between conjunctive nomenclature and 取代命名法, 优选IUPAC名称 are
formed by using 取代命名法 (including 倍增命名法 and 骨架置换
nomenclature when the conditions for their use are fulfilled) (see P-51).
Examples:
萘-2,3-di乙酸
2,2′-(萘-2,3-diyl)di乙酸 (PIN)
苯-1,3,5-tri乙酸
2,2′,2′′-(苯-1,3,5-triyl)tri乙酸 (PIN)
2-(3-羟基丙基)喹啉-3-乙酸
[2-(3-羟基丙基)quinolin-3-yl]乙酸 (PIN)
(a 羧酸 is senior to an alcohol)
1-(2-carboxy乙基)萘-2,3-di乙酸
3-[2,3-bis(carboxy甲基)naphthalen-1-yl]propanoic acid (PIN)
P-52 SELECTING PREFERRED IUPAC NAMES AND PRESELECTED NAMES (see
P-12.2) FOR PARENT HYDRIDES
For naming the 母体氢化物 described in Chapter P-2, when only one method is described, the resulting single
names are naturally 优选IUPAC名称. When more than one method is recommended for generating the names of
母体氢化物, 优选IUPAC名称, and in some cases 预选名称, must be chosen. Some 保留名称
are used as 优选IUPAC名称 and as names for use in 通用命名法.

P-52.1.5 Heteromonocyclic noncarbon Hantzsch-Widman 母体氢化物
The final 'e' in Hantzsch-Widman名称 is required in 优选IUPAC名称; it is still optional in general
nomenclature. In the 1979 Rules (ref. 1), the final 'e' of a Hantzsch-Widman name was omitted when there was no
nitrogen in the ring; in the 1993 Guide (ref. 2) this omission was made optional.
P-52.1.5.1 Preselected names for homogeneous hetero单环母体氢化物 consisting of ten or fewer ring
members are Hantzsch-Widman名称 (see P-22.2.2). 骨架置换 ('a') names are 预选名称 for

homogeneous hetero单环母体氢化物 with more than ten ring members (see P-22.2.3). Alternative names,
which 可用于通用命名法, are those formed by using the prefix 'cyclo' (see P-22.2.5).
Examples:
pentazolidine (预选名称)
cyclopentaazane
hexagerminane (预选名称)
cyclohexa锗烷
dodecasilacyclododecane (预选名称; see P-12.2)
cyclododeca硅烷
P-52.1.5.2 Heterogeneous hetero单环母体氢化物 consisting of alternating 杂原子
Preselected names for heterogeneous hetero单环母体氢化物 with ten or fewer ring members and consisting
of alternating 杂原子, i.e., [ab] , are Hantzsch-Widman名称 (see P-22.2.2). 骨架置换 ('a') names
n
(see P-22.2.3) are 预选名称 for heterogeneous monoheterocyclic 母体氢化物 with alternating 杂原子
with more than ten ring members. Alternative names for use only in 通用命名法 are those formed by using the
prefix 'cyclo' (see P-52.1).
Examples:
1,3,5,2,4,6-triphosphatriborinane (预选名称)
cyclotribora磷烷
1,3,5,7,9,11,13-heptaoxa-2,4,6,8,10,12,14-heptasilacyclotetradecane (预选名称)
cycloheptasil氧杂环己烷
P-52.1.6 Heterocyclic noncarbon von Baeyer and 螺环化合物
P-52.1.6.1 Preselected names for von Baeyer compounds and for 螺环化合物 having only monocyclic components
and consisting entirely of 杂原子 of the same kind are names formed by citing the appropriate prefix, such as
'bicyclo', 'spiro', etc., and descriptor enclosed within square brackets followed by a numerical prefix for the total
number of 杂原子 and the name of the mononuclear 母体氢化物; alternative names for use in general
nomenclature are formed by 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature (see P-23.4 and P-24.2.4.2).

Examples:

dodecasilatricyclo[5.3.1.12,6]dodecane
P-52.1.6.2 Preselected names for von Baeyer compounds and for 螺环化合物 having only monocyclic components
and consisting of alternating 杂原子, i.e., [ab] , are formed by citing the appropriate prefix, such as 'bicyclo',
n
'spiro', etc., and a descriptor enclosed within square brackets followed by the number of the ('a') 杂原子, the
骨架置换 name of the ('a') atom, and the 骨架置换 name of the ('b') 杂原子 (see P-24.2.4.3);
alternative names for use in 通用命名法 are those formed by 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature.
Examples:
spiro[5.5]pentasil氧杂环己烷 (预选名称)
1,3,5,7,9,11-hexaoxa-2,4,6,8,10-pentasilaspiro[5.5]undecane
tricyclo[3.3.1.13,7]tetrasil氧杂环己烷 (预选名称)
2,4,6,8,9,10-hexaoxa-1,3,5,7-tetrasilaadamantane
2,4,6,8,9,10-hexaoxa-1,3,5,7-tetrasilatricyclo[3.3.1.13,7]decane
P-52.1.7 Preselected names for homogeneous and heterogeneous heterobi- and heteropolycyclic fused ring systems are
formed by fusion nomenclature principles (see P-25.3.2.4). Alternative names for use in 通用命名法 are
formed by prefixing the appropriate 'a' prefixes in front of the name of the hydrocarbon fused ring system.
Examples:

1H,4H-octaarsapentalene (numbering shown)
[1,3,5,2,4,6]triazatriborinino[1,2-a][1,3,5,2,4,6]triazatriborinine (预选名称)

H N-NH .

P-52.2.2.2 Preferred IUPAC names for heteromonocyclic rings with no more than ten ring members are Hantzsch-
Widman names, including the locants '1,2' and '1,3'. The 保留名称 '噁唑', 'is噁唑', '噻唑', and
'iso噻唑' are allowed in 通用命名法.
Although the final 'e' in Hantzsch-Widman名称 is required in 优选IUPAC名称; it is still optional in
通用命名法. In the 1979 Rules (ref. 1), the final 'e' of a Hantzsch-Widman name was omitted when there
was no nitrogen in the ring; in the 1993 Guide (ref. 2) this omission was made optional.
Examples:
1,2-噁唑 (PIN)
is噁唑
1,3-噻唑 (PIN)
噻唑
P-52.2.3 Un饱和杂单环化合物 with more than ten ring members.
Preferred IUPAC names for 不饱和 monocyclic compounds with more than ten ring members derived from
cycloalkanes and modified by 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature are formed by changing the 'ane' ending of the


### 第 450 页

饱和 heteromonocycle to 'ene', 'adiene', etc. (see P-31.1.1). [n]Annulene names may be used in general
nomenclature (see P-31.1.3.2) for the heteromonocycle itself; however, the name '轮烯' cannot be used to
designate these heterocyclic compounds as components in fusion nomenclature.
Example:
1-azacyclotrideca-2,4,6,8,10,12-hexaene (PIN)
1-azacyclotridecine [优选IUPAC名称
for the principal component in a fusion name (see also P-25.2.2.1.2)
and 1-azacyclotridecino as an attached component (see P-25.3.2.2.2)]
1H-1-aza[13]轮烯
P-52.2.4 Preferred IUPAC names in fusion nomenclature
P-52.2.4.1 Five-membered ring requirement
Fusion nomenclature gives 优选IUPAC名称 only to compounds having at least two rings of at least five or more
members. This requirement is not necessarily applied in 通用命名法, in which names such as
cyclopropa苯 and cyclobuta苯 can be used. When fusion names are not allowed, 不饱和 von Baeyer
ring system names are 优选IUPAC名称 (see P-31.1.4.2).
This is a change from the recommendation in the 1998 publication on fused ring nomenclature (see FR-0, ref. 4) and
the 1993 Guide (ref. 2) where no restriction was placed on the size of the two rings that could be used in a fused ring
system. For 优选IUPAC名称 a fusion name can only be used when at least two rings of five or more
members are present; this is consistent with recommendations in the 1979 edition (ref. 1). In 通用命名法
there is no restriction on the size of rings in a fused ring system.
Examples:
1H-cyclopropa苯
bicyclo[4.1.0]hepta-1,3,5-triene (PIN)
cyclobuta苯
bicyclo[4.2.0]octa-1,3,5,7-tetraene (PIN)
P-52.2.4.2 Heteromonocycles as components in fusion names
Heteromonocycles having more than ten ring members and the 最大非累积双键数
whose names are denoted by the 'ine' ending described in P-22.2.4 are used as 母体 components as well as attached
components in preferred IUPAC fusion names. 'Annulene' names modified by 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature
(see P-52.2.3) are not recommended for generating names of heterocyclic fusion compounds.
Examples:
9H-dibenzo[g,p][1,3,6,9,12,15,18]heptaoxacycloicosine (PIN; see P-25.3.6.1)


### 第 451 页

[1,4,7,10]tetraoxacyclohexadecino[13,12-b:14,15-b′]di吡啶 (PIN; see P-25.3.7.1)
P-52.2.4.3 Multi母体 fused ring systems with three or more inter母体 components
When two (or more) possible 母体 components are separated by an odd number of inter母体 components and these
are ordered symmetrically with respect to their component rings (but not necessarily with their fusion locants), the
whole system is treated as a multi母体 system. In P-25.3.7.3, second- and higher-order inter母体 components are
named using the 倍乘前缀 'di', 'tri', etc., or 'bis', 'tris', etc. Appropriate locants are assigned to inter母体
components, unprimed and primed for first-order inter母体 components, double primed for second-order inter母体
components, triple primed for third-order inter母体 components, and so on.
Example:
benzo[1′′,2′′:3,4;4′′,5′′:3′,4′]dicyclobuta[1,2-b:1′,2′-c′]di呋喃 (PIN)
When symmetry permits grouping of inter母体 components and 母体 components, such groupings can be formed
and cited as such using the prefixes 'bis', 'tris', etc. to denote groups that are enclosed within 母体heses. Unprimed
locants only are 与...一起使用in such groupings. This method is often encountered and may be used in general
nomenclature.
Example:
benzo[1′′,2′′:3,4;4′′,5′′,3′,4′]dicyclobuta[1,2-c:1′,2′-c′]di呋喃 (PIN)
benzo[1′′,2′′:3,4;4′′,5′′:3′,4′]bis(cyclobuta[1,2-c]呋喃)
P-52.2.4.4 Limitations to fusion nomenclature
The fusion principles described in P-25.1 through P-25.3 apply to pairs of components. It is not possible by these
principles to name a system in which a third component is ortho- and peri-fused to two components that are themselves
ortho- or ortho- and peri-fused together. Hence, when a third component is ortho- and peri-fused to two components
that are themselves ortho- or ortho- and peri-fused together, the following procedures are applied to generate a
优选IUPAC名称.
P-52.2.4.4.1 Selection of a less senior 母体 ring or ring system
P-52.2.4.4.1.1 A less preferred 母体 ring or ring system component is selected that will permit a fusion name. Second
and third choice of 母体 rings or ring system 母体 components may be chosen according to the seniority order for
selecting the senior ring or ring system as the 母体 for naming the fused ring system (see P-25.3.2.4).
Examples:
cyclobuta[1,7]indeno[5,6-b]萘 (PIN)
Explanation: Anthracene cannot be selected as senior 母体 component; 萘, not 茚, is next in seniority
order for selection as a 母体 component.
10-azacyclobuta[1,7]indeno[5,6-b]蒽 (PIN)


### 第 452 页

Explanation: Neither 喹啉 nor 吡啶 can be used as the senior 母体 component because neither 萘
nor 蒽, respectively, can be used as the senior attached component; therefore 骨架置换 ('a')
nomenclature must be used (see P-25.5.1); and since the preferred hydrocarbon tetracene cannot be used as the 母体
hydrocarbon to which skeletal ('a') replacement can be applied (see P-52.2.4.4.2), the next senior component,
蒽, is chosen as the 母体 hydrocarbon component.
P-52.2.4.4.1.2 It should be noted that benzo heterocycles are considered as one component, thus permitting the
construction of fusion names for ring systems that could not otherwise be named by fusion principles.
Example:
2H-[1,3]benzodioxino[6′,5′,4′:10,5,6]anthra[2,3-b]氮杂卓 (PIN)
Explanation: A normal fusion name is not possible when the four components 氮杂卓, 蒽, 1,3-dioxine, and
苯 are treated individually; therefore, the use of a benzo name component is necessary; 1-benz氮杂卓 cannot be
the 母体 ring because this would require breaking of the attached component that has a 保留名称, anthra, which is
not allowed, see P-25.3.5.
P-52.2.4.4.2 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature
When the fusion principles discussed in P-25.1 through P-25.3 apply, no 骨架置换 ('a') name is
recommended. This procedure is valid only for cases described here in P-52.2.4.4.2.1.
P-52.2.4.4.2.1 If the corresponding hydrocarbon fused ring system can be named by fusion principles or has a retained
name, then 杂原子 are identified by 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature using the appropriate 'a' prefixes (see
P-22.2.3). The numbering of the fused hydrocarbon system is not altered by the 'a' prefixes.
Examples:
1,2,3,4,5,6-hexaazacyclopenta[cd]pentalene (PIN)
1,3a1,4,9-tetraazaphenalene (PIN)
5H,12H-2,3,4a,7a,9,10,11a,14a-octaazadicyclopenta[ij:i′j′]benzo[1,2-f:4,5-f′]diazulene (PIN)
P-52.2.4.4.2.2 If the fused ring system can only be named using skeletal ('a') replacement nomenclature, any
杂原子 in bridges are also named using 骨架置换 ('a') nomenclature. The replacement terms are cited
at the front of the corresponding bridged fused hydrocarbon ring system. Alternatively, the 杂原子ic bridge may be
named by an appropriate compound or complex bridge prefix.


### 第 453 页

Examples:

P-52.2.4.4.3 Bridging nomenclature. A bridged fused system (see Section P-25.4) is used to generate names for
structures that cannot be named by normal fusion nomenclature. A properly formed fusion name is first created; then
additional rings are created by using bridges.
Examples:
12,19:13,18-di(metheno)dinaphtho[2,3-a:2′,3′-o]pentaphene (PIN)
